<div class="article-card__picture"><img alt="The Top Five Vape Pens for Summer" class="img-responsive lazyload article-card__picture__image lazyaspectratio" data-aspectratio="1.5" data-sizes="auto" data-srcset="//cdn.shopify.com/s/files/1/1252/6591/articles/vacations-summer-fun-smiling-group-friends-happy-youth-beach-day-millennials_t20_v3EGYO_2_{width}x.jpg?v=1556729818" src="//cdn.shopify.com/s/files/1/1252/6591/articles/vacations-summer-fun-smiling-group-friends-happy-youth-beach-day-millennials_t20_v3EGYO_2_300x.jpg?v=1556729818" srcset="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" style="height: 155.333px;"/></div>
<h2 class="article-card__title">The Top Five Vape Pens for Summer</h2>
</a>
<p class="text-muted article-card__meta">
<small><time datetime="2019-06-27">June 27, 2019</time></small>
</p>
<p class="article-card__excerpt">
</p><p>Summer is right around the corner, so it’s the perfect time to upgrade your old vape pen for something more suitable for the season. Summer means hot weather and less clothes, so you’re going to want something sleek and portable, without the overheating factor. Here are some of our best vapes pens for summer.</p>
<p></p>
<p><a class="btn article-card__readmore" href="/blogs/news/the-top-five-vape-pens-for-summer">Read more</a></p>
</div>

<a href="/blogs/news/the-lighter-types-you-need-for-every-occasion">
<div class="article-card__picture"><img alt="The Lighter Types You Need for Every Occasion" class="img-responsive lazyload article-card__picture__image lazyaspectratio" data-aspectratio="1.0" data-sizes="auto" data-srcset="//cdn.shopify.com/s/files/1/1252/6591/articles/meetings_t20_Zza6aR_{width}x.jpg?v=1556729515" src="//cdn.shopify.com/s/files/1/1252/6591/articles/meetings_t20_Zza6aR_300x.jpg?v=1556729515" srcset="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" style="height: 233px;"/></div>
<h2 class="article-card__title">The Lighter Types You Need for Every Occasion</h2>
</a>
<p class="text-muted article-card__meta">
<small><time datetime="2019-06-20">June 20, 2019</time></small>
</p>
<p class="article-card__excerpt">
<span>While many people associate lighters with a Zippo or Bic lighter, there are actually specific lighter types that are made to fit each of your lighter needs. Below we explain the various lighter types we carry in our </span><a href="https://www.lighterusa.com/">store</a><span> and when to use them.</span>
</p>

<div class="article-card__picture"><img alt="Nicotine Salts Pens: A Better Bang for Your Buck" class="img-responsive lazyload article-card__picture__image lazyaspectratio" data-aspectratio="1.5" data-sizes="auto" data-srcset="//cdn.shopify.com/s/files/1/1252/6591/articles/swagg-sauce-salt-nerd-nicotine-e-liquid-nic-salts-under-50-vape-juice-lighter-usa_978_450x_2x_71fe179b-56eb-4b04-9e3a-007164e7ed01_{width}x.jpg?v=1555960259" src="//cdn.shopify.com/s/files/1/1252/6591/articles/swagg-sauce-salt-nerd-nicotine-e-liquid-nic-salts-under-50-vape-juice-lighter-usa_978_450x_2x_71fe179b-56eb-4b04-9e3a-007164e7ed01_300x.jpg?v=1555960259" srcset="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" style="height: 155.333px;"/></div>
<h2 class="article-card__title">Nicotine Salts Pens: A Better Bang for Your Buck</h2>
</a>
<p class="text-muted article-card__meta">
<small><time datetime="2019-06-13">June 13, 2019</time></small>
</p>
<p class="article-card__excerpt">
<span>Though they are a newer addition to the vaping world, nicotine salts seem to be the talk of the town in the vaping community lately. These salt pens are growing in popularity, particularly amongst those who are seeking to satisfy their cigarette cravings </span><a href="https://www.ecigarettedirect.co.uk/ashtray-blog/2018/04/nicotine-salts-vapers.html">without actually smoking a cigarette</a><span>. </span>

<script>
  (function($, undefined){
    $(window).load(function(){
      var popupSelector = '#NewsletterPopup';
      if (!Cookies.get('mcpu_shown')) {
        setTimeout(function(){
          $.magnificPopup.open({
            items: {
              src: popupSelector,
              type: 'inline'
            },
            midClick: true,
            removalDelay: 300,
            callbacks: {
              beforeOpen: function() {
                this.st.mainClass = $(popupSelector).data('mfpEffect');
              },
              afterClose: function() {
                if (!Cookies.get('mcpu_shown')) {
                  Cookies.set('mcpu_shown', 1, {expires: 3});
                }
              }
            }
          });
        }, $(popupSelector).data('mfpDelay'));
      }
    });

    // Submit form
    var $form = $('#NewsletterPopupForm');
    var $msg  = $('#NewsletterPopupMsg');

    $form.on('submit', function(event){
      event.preventDefault();
      $.get(
        $form.attr('action'),
        $form.serialize(),
        function(response){
          $msg.html(response.msg).find('a').attr('target', '_blank');
          if (response.result === 'success') {
            $msg.addClass('msg--success');
            $form.slideUp('fast');
            Cookies.set('mcpu_shown', 1, {expires: 365});
          } else {
            $msg.addClass('msg--error');
          }

          $msg.slideDown('fast');
        },
        'json'
      );
    });
  })(jQuery);
</script>
